===== f4743 usize=1603
CommonModule = {}

function CommonModule.Clone(object)
	local lookup_table = {}
	local function _copy(object)
		if type(object) ~= "table" then
			return object
		elseif lookup_table[object] then
			return lookup_table[object]
		end
		local newObject = {}
		lookup_table[object] = newObject
		for key, value in pairs(object) do
			newObject[_copy(key)] = _copy(value)
		end
		return setmetatable(newObject, getmetatable(object))
	end
	return _copy(object)
end

local s_register_class = {}
function CommonModule.GetRegisterClass( classname )
	return s_register_class[classname]
end

function CommonModule.RegisterClass( classname, cls)
	assert(s_register_class[classname] == nil, string.format("classname[%s]is exist", classname))
	s_register_class[classname] = cls
end

function CommonModule.Class( name, super)
	local cls = nil

	if super then
		cls = CommonModule.Clone(super)
		cls.super = super
	else
		cls = { OnInit = function() end, Destroy = function() end}
	end

	cls.__index = cls

	local create = nil
	create = function(instance, c, ...)
		if c.super then
            create(instance, c.super, ...)
        end

        if c.OnInit then
            c.OnInit(instance, ...)
        end
	end

	function cls.New(...)
        local instance = setmetatable({}, cls)
        create(instance, cls, ...)
        return instance
    end

    CommonModule.RegisterClass(name, cls)
    
    return cls
end

function math.clamp(value, min, max)
	if value < min then return min end
	if value > max then return max end

	return value
end
===== f4750 usize=2089
local BaseCamera = require(script.Parent.BaseCamera)
local WorkSpace = game:GetService("WorkSpace")
local Players = game:GetService('Players')
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local ClassicCamera = CommonModule.Class("ClassicCamera", BaseCamera)
local MouseService = game:GetService("MouseService")

function ClassicCamera:OnInit(...)
	BaseCamera.OnInit(self, ...)
	self.m_lastEyePos = Vector3.New(0, 0, 0)
	self.m_lastQuaterion = Quaternion.FromEuler(0, 0, 0)
end

function ClassicCamera:Update()
	local currentCamera = WorkSpace.CurrentCamera
	local camType = currentCamera.CameraType

	local desteyepos = nil
	local destquaterion = nil

	if camType == Enum.CameraType.Fixed then
		desteyepos = self.m_lastEyePos
		destquaterion = self.m_lastQuaterion
	elseif camType == Enum.CameraType.Attach then
		local subpos, subeuler = self:GetSubjectPosAndEuler()
		local rotation = self:GetRotation()
		local quaterion = Quaternion.FromEuler(rotation.x, subeuler.y+180, 0)
		local lookdir = quaterion:RotateToDir(Vector3.New(0, 0, 1))
		local eyepos = player:EyePos(subpos, -lookdir, self:GetEyeDistance())
		desteyepos = eyepos
		destquaterion = quaterion
	elseif 	camType == Enum.CameraType.Watch or 
		  	camType == Enum.CameraType.Track or  
		  	camType == Enum.CameraType.Follow or 
		  	camType == Enum.CameraType.Custom 
	then
		local subpos = self:GetSubjectPosAndEuler()
		local quaterion = self:GetQuaternion()
		local lookdir = quaterion:RotateToDir(Vector3.New(0, 0, 1))
		local eyepos = player:EyePos(subpos, -lookdir, self:GetEyeDistance())
		desteyepos = eyepos
		destquaterion = quaterion
	elseif camType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
		-- Scriptable 摄像机控制权交给用户自定义脚本控制
	end
	
	if destquaterion then
		self:SetQuaternion(destquaterion)
		self.m_lastQuaterion = destquaterion
	end

	if desteyepos then
		self:SetPosition(desteyepos)
		self.m_lastEyePos = desteyepos
	end
end

function ClassicCamera:GetCameraType()
	return Enum.CameraType.Custom.Value;
end

return ClassicCamera
===== f4753 usize=1154
local WorldService = game:GetService("WorldService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local State = CommonModule.GetRegisterClass("State")
local AttackState = CommonModule.Class("AttackState", State)

AttackState.FROM_STATE = { "IdleState" }

local ATTACK_GAP = 1000

function AttackState:OnInit(...)
	State.OnInit(self, ...)
	self.m_attackTime = 0
end

function AttackState:checkAttack()
	local curtime = RunService:CurrentMilliSecondTimeStamp()
	if curtime - self.m_attackTime < ATTACK_GAP then
		return
	end

	local result = self.m_host:doPick()
	print("checkAttack pickType", result.pickType, result.actor)
	local pickType = result.pickType

	if pickType == 2 then
		self.m_attackTime = curtime
		self.m_host:PlayAnim("100105")
		WorldService:AttackTarget(result.actor)
	end
end

function AttackState:EnterState()
	self:checkAttack()
end

function AttackState:LeaveState()
	self.m_attackTime = 0
	self.m_host:StopAnim("100105")
end

function AttackState:OnUpdate(dt)
	if not self.m_host:isLeftClickDown() then
		return "IdleState"
	else
		self:checkAttack()
	end
end

return AttackState
===== f4754 usize=3488
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local WorldService = game:GetService("WorldService")
local BlockService = game:GetService("BlockService")

local State = CommonModule.GetRegisterClass("State")
local DigState = CommonModule.Class("DigState", State)

DigState.FROM_STATE = { "IdleState" }

local BLOCK_SIZE = 100
local DIG_NEED_TIME = 1000

function DigState:EnterState()
	self.m_enterStateTime = RunService:CurrentMilliSecondTimeStamp()
	self:checkDoDig()
end

function DigState:checkDoDig()
	local result = self.m_host:doPick()
	print("checkDoDig pickType", result.pickType, result.block)
	local pickType = result.pickType
	
	self.m_curPickType = pickType
	self.m_isDigging = false
	self.m_isAttack = false
	self.m_needQuit  = false


	if pickType == 1 then
		self:DigBlock(result.block, result.face)
	else
		self.m_needQuit =  true
		self.m_isDigging = false
		self.m_isAttack = false
	end
end

local function handleDigDone( self )
	--print("handleDigDone")
	BlockService:DropBlockAsItem(game.WorkSpace, self.m_curDigBlock, 1, 1, 0)

	local blockpos = Vector3.New(self.m_curDigBlock.x*100, self.m_curDigBlock.y*100, self.m_curDigBlock.z*100)
	BlockService:DestroyBlock(game.WorkSpace,blockpos, true)
	--BlockService:SetBlock(game.WorkSpace,self.m_curDigBlock, 0, 0, 3, true)
end

local function _GetBlockCenterPos( block )
	return block*BLOCK_SIZE + Vector3.New(BLOCK_SIZE/2, BLOCK_SIZE/2, BLOCK_SIZE/2)
end

function DigState:playEffectSound( soundpath )
	if self.m_soundNode == nil then
		self.m_soundNode = SandboxNode.new("SandboxSound", self.m_host.m_charater)
	end
	self.m_soundNode.FixPos = _GetBlockCenterPos(self.m_curDigBlock)
	self.m_soundNode.SoundPath = soundpath
	self.m_soundNode.IsLoop = false;
	self.m_soundNode:PlaySound()
end

local function handlePlayDigSound( self )
	self:playEffectSound("sandboxSysId://sounds/blocks/grass1.ogg")
end

local function handleUpdateCrackEffect( self )

end

function DigState:updateDigState()
	if not self.m_isDigging then return end

	local curtime = RunService:CurrentMilliSecondTimeStamp()
	if self.m_digDoneTime > 0 and curtime >= self.m_digDoneTime then
		handleDigDone(self)
		self:checkDoDig()
	else
		handlePlayDigSound(self)
		handleUpdateCrackEffect(self)
	end
end

function DigState:DigBlock(block, face)
	--assert("wait do")
	
	self:PerformDigAnim()
	self.m_isDigging = true
	self.m_curDigBlock = block
	self.m_face = face
	self.m_digDoneTime = RunService:CurrentMilliSecondTimeStamp() + DIG_NEED_TIME
end


function DigState:PerformDigAnim()
	self.m_playAnimBeginTime = RunService:CurrentMilliSecondTimeStamp()
	self.m_host:PlayAnim("100105")
end

function DigState:LeaveState()
	self.m_digDoneTime = -1
	self.m_isDigging = false
	self.m_isAttack = false
	self.m_playAnimBeginTime = -1
	self.m_host:StopAnim("100105")
end

function DigState:IsChangeDigBlock()
	if not self.m_isDigging then return false end

	local result = self.m_host:doPick()
	if result.pickType ~= 1 or result.block ~= self.m_curDigBlock then
		return true
	end

	return false
end

function DigState:OnUpdate(dt)
	if self.m_needQuit then
		return "IdleState"
	end

	if not self.m_host:IsCanDig() then
		return "IdleState"
	end

	if not self:IsChangeDigBlock() then --如果挖的方块变化了，则重新走开始挖的逻辑
		self:checkDoDig()
	else
		self:updateDigState()
	end
end

return DigState
